## D-039 — D-038 Completion Reconciliation and D-034 Stage 5f Final Correction and Security Review

- **Status:** Accepted
- **Date proposed:** September 2, 2026
- **Date accepted:** September 2, 2026
- **Context:** D-034 remains the historical foundation decision for the Portal Invitation / Account Activation contract; D-035 recorded Stage 3; D-036 recorded Stage 4; D-037 recorded the Stage 5a activation contract (Stage 5's own sub-stages 5b–5f); D-038 recorded the fragment-transport security remediation (its own sub-stages D-038a–d). D-038 §10/§11 left "D-034 Stage 5 remains **FAIL** pending D-038b through D-038d," and no Decisions-Log entry had recorded D-038b/c/d as executed or D-034 Stage 5f as performed. This entry is that reconciliation, plus the fresh, final D-034 Stage 5f Correction and Security Review Pass over the complete Stage 5 + D-038 implementation as it stands on `main`@`06f04df`. It was produced on branch `fix/phase-3-portal-activation-stage-5f-reconciliation` (branched from `main`@`06f04df`, working tree clean) under an authorized eight-path file scope (§13). It does not amend or supersede D-034, D-035, D-036, D-037, or D-038 except for the single "Stage 5 remains FAIL" status line named in D-038 §Effect, which accepted D-039 supersedes once this accepted entry is committed and merged into `main` (§11).

  **1. Purpose and scope.** This entry: (a) records D-038b and D-038c as implemented and merged; (b) records the September 1, 2026 D-038d review and identifies exactly which of its effects are independently visible in the merged diff and which are not; (c) records the F2 and F3 code/test corrections this branch makes; (d) records the accepted F4 contract correction (documentation-only, no code change); (e) records F6 (a pre-existing Windows CRLF checkout/tooling condition) and F7 (the pre-existing Resend caret range, outside this branch's scope) honestly and without overstating validation; (f) keeps the Hostinger and Resend deployment gates explicitly open; (g) supersedes D-038's "Stage 5 remains FAIL" status once this accepted entry is committed and merged into `main`; and (h) states the precise gate on D-034 Stage 6 becoming development-unblocked. It authorizes no schema change, no migration, no dependency change, no environment-file change, and no Task Board change.

  **2. D-038b and D-038c: implemented and merged.** D-038b (authoritative fragment route relocation, dead-code removal, `buildActivationUrl` fragment shape, `next.config.ts` matcher narrowing, `ActivationForm` `window.location.hash` transport) and D-038c (Stage-5e live-E2E and artifact-safety-harness adaptation to the fragment flow) were implemented in commit `9816910` — together with the D-038a documentation entry itself (+122 log lines) — and merged to `main` through PR #67, present on `main`@`06f04df`. The commit is titled "test(web): add portal activation live evidence"; its content is broader than that title. Reconciled against D-038 §8's inventory, the actual `9816910` diff: `apps/web/src/app/activate/[token]/page.tsx` and `.../page.test.tsx` **deleted**; `apps/web/src/app/activate/[token]/_components/ActivationForm.tsx` and `.../ActivationForm.test.tsx` **relocated** to `apps/web/src/app/activate/_components/`; `apps/web/src/app/activate/page.tsx` and `page.test.tsx` **new** (static tokenless shell); `apps/web/src/features/invitations/resend-adapter.ts` `buildActivationUrl` template changed to `${base}/activate#token=${encodeURIComponent(rawToken)}`, with its one shape assertion in `resend-adapter.test.ts` updated; `apps/web/next.config.ts` `headers()` `source` narrowed from `/activate/:token*` to `/activate`; `apps/web/src/features/activation/service.ts` and `service.test.ts` **removed** `getActivationPageState` and the `ActivationPageState` type as dead code (confirmed by search: neither symbol appears in any executable code on `06f04df`, only in comments and this log); `apps/web/e2e/activation.spec.ts` **new** (headers + live token-absence gate + full browser happy path, `test.use({ trace:'off', screenshot:'off', video:'off' })`); `apps/web/e2e/support/artifact-safety-probe.spec.ts` **new** (tag-isolated `@expected-failure-probe`, one real Continue click added for the fragment flow); `apps/web/e2e/support/verify-artifact-safety.ts` **new** (the controlled expected-failure harness); `apps/web/e2e/support/run-e2e.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/support/test-database.ts`, `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/web/package.json`, `apps/web/.env.example`, and `docs/HERITAGE_V3_ENVIRONMENT_CONFIGURATION.md` adjusted for the isolated E2E server and the artifact-safety harness. The token-absence assertion in `activation.spec.ts` (`expect(eligibleBody.includes(rawToken)).toBe(false)`) is present, unskipped, un-inverted, and not marked expected — its comment states it is expected to have flipped from its pre-remediation reproducible failure to a genuine pass under the fragment route.

  **3. D-038d: the September 1, 2026 review, and what is independently visible in the merged diff.** Per the prior session's recorded history, a D-038d Correction and Security Review Pass was performed on branch `feat/phase-3-portal-activation-live-evidence` on September 1, 2026; it found and corrected stale rate-limit documentation comments, among other checks, and its resulting changes were included in PR #67 and merged at `06f04df`. **Independently visible in the `9816910` merged diff** (not taken on trust): `apps/web/src/features/activation/rate-limit.ts`'s `checkSourceRateLimit` doc comment and `apps/web/src/features/activation/http.ts`'s module header were both changed from wording naming "both POST routes, the GET page" as callers/order participants to "the two POST routes only … the GET page performs no rate-limit check of any kind (D-038 Section 3)" — a review-driven staleness correction consistent with a dedicated D-038d pass. **Not independently provable from repository evidence alone** (accepted here as prior-session history, not as repo-verified fact): that a discrete D-038d review pass ran as its own step rather than the corrections being made inline during D-038b/c (there is no separate commit, no separate PR body, and no D-038d Decisions-Log entry); and that the full D-037 §17 / D-038 §9 validation battery (`pnpm build`, `test:integration`, `test:e2e`, `test:e2e:verify-artifact-safety`, the live Cache-Control / token-absence / Prisma-`upsert`-atomicity evidence) was executed with passing results on that branch (no run logs are committed, consistent with this repository's "local evidence only, never in the tree" practice). This entry's own §10 supplies a fresh, independently-executed validation record on the reconciliation branch tip to close that gap for Stage 5f.

  **4. F2 correction (this branch) — `apps/web/src/features/activation/source.ts`.** The `HeadersLike` interface's doc comment previously justified its narrow shape by reference to "`/activate/[token]`'s Server Component [which] has no `Request` object available at all" — a rationale made obsolete by D-038 §3, which removed that route and its server-side source check. The comment now states the accurate current reason: `resolveRequestSource` is called only from the two activation POST route handlers (`app/api/activation/continue/route.ts`, `app/api/activation/activate/route.ts`), each passing its request's own WHATWG `Headers` (`request.headers`); narrowing the parameter to just the headers view it actually reads keeps the resolver independent of any framework request type and trivially unit-testable with a plain `{ get }` stub (`source.test.ts`), a deliberate adaptation of D-037 §12's illustrative `Request`-taking signature, not a silent deviation. In the same file (F2-adjacent, same authorized path, comment-only), the module header's Section-12 deployment-gate summary was corrected to D-038 §6's narrowed scope — (a) verified Hostinger reverse-proxy trusted-source-header behavior, applicable only to SOURCE-dimension rate limiting on the two activation POST routes, and (b) confirmation that proxy/origin request-body logging is disabled for `/api/activation/continue` and `/api/activation/activate` specifically — while keeping its conclusion ("activation must not be exposed in staging or production regardless of this stage's completion") unchanged. This change is comment-only: no exported type, signature, or runtime behavior is altered, confirmed by an unchanged `pnpm typecheck` and unit-test result and by a diff that touches only comment lines.

  **5. F3 correction (this branch) — activation-URL test doubles.** D-038 §8 identified a set of test doubles still emitting the pre-remediation path-shaped `http://localhost:3000/activate/<token>` activation URL and ruled them "not required to change for correctness … recommended for shape consistency." This branch closes that gap consistently across the exact six files: `apps/web/src/features/activation/service.integration.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/invitations/service.integration.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/invitations/service.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/admin/clients/[id]/_components/PortalInvitationPanel.test.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/api/clients/[id]/invitation/send/route.test.ts`, and `apps/web/src/features/invitations/http.test.ts`. Every path-shaped literal, template, and regex is replaced with the accepted fragment shape `http://localhost:3000/activate#token=<encoded-token>` (the `${encodeURIComponent(rawToken)}` mock templates match the real `buildActivationUrl` exactly; opaque fixture identifiers such as `mock-token` / `secret-raw-token` are carried through unchanged after `#token=`). `apps/web/src/features/activation/service.integration.test.ts`'s local `extractToken` helper is rewritten from `manualInvitationUrl.split('/').pop()!` to a fragment parser (`new URL(manualInvitationUrl).hash` → `/#token=([^&]+)$/` → `decodeURIComponent`) — the identical `#token=` contract `ActivationForm` and `e2e/activation.spec.ts` consume. Helpers are kept local and simple; no production abstraction is introduced for test-only parsing. All existing assertions and test intent are preserved: only URL string shapes and their paired equality assertions changed; the `PortalInvitationPanel` "never persists / never logs the manual link" security assertions still key on the unchanged token-identifier substring (`secret-raw-token`), so their meaning is intact.

  **6. F4 — accepted contract correction (documentation-only; no code change).** D-037 §3's literal text describes the activation password-validation `details` array as `{ path: 'password', message }` objects for all three messages, including "Passwords do not match." The implemented behavior — `activateSchema`'s `.refine(..., { path: ['confirmPassword'] })`, and `ActivationForm` rendering `fieldErrors.confirmPassword` beneath the confirm-password field — attributes the mismatch error to `confirmPassword`, which is the correct field-level UX (the mismatch is a property of the confirmation input, and the user's fix is to that input) and is already covered by tests. **This entry supersedes D-037 §3 on this one point:** the passwords-mismatch error's `details` entry uses `{ path: 'confirmPassword', message: 'Passwords do not match.' }`; the two length errors (`Password must be at least 12 characters.`, `Password must not exceed 200 characters.`) remain on `{ path: 'password', message }` exactly as D-037 §3 states. No code changes for F4 — the implementation already matches this corrected contract. Anti-enumeration is unaffected: password validation is the single deliberately-specific error class in the activation flow (D-037 §3), and it never discloses invitation existence or state.

  **7. F6 — pre-existing Windows CRLF/LF-mix checkout/tooling condition.** On the local Windows checkout, the canonical `pnpm format:check` (`prettier --check .`) fails, reporting `Code style issues found in 302 files` (exit 1) — essentially every tracked source file — because `git config core.autocrlf` is `true`, there is no `.gitattributes`, and `.prettierrc.json` sets no `endOfLine` (so Prettier's default `endOfLine: "lf"` flags every working-tree file materialized with CRLF). The working tree is in fact a **CRLF/LF mix**: most files are CRLF, while a handful (`.prettierrc.json`, `apps/web/tsconfig.json`, `apps/web/eslint.config.mjs`, `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `apps/web/src/app/api/health/route.ts`) are LF (`git ls-files --eol` shows `w/lf` for those), so a forced `--end-of-line crlf` check symmetrically fails on exactly those 7 LF files. This condition is pre-existing, branch-independent, and unrelated to Stage 5, D-038, or this reconciliation; none of the flagged files is touched by this branch. **This entry does not claim the canonical `pnpm format:check` passed** — its actual failing result is recorded verbatim in §10. As a separate diagnostic, never presented as the canonical command, `prettier --check --end-of-line auto .` (which honors each file's own existing line endings and therefore isolates genuine content issues from line-ending noise) **passes repo-wide, exit 0** (`All matched files use Prettier code style!`) — direct proof that the repository has zero content-level Prettier deviations and the canonical failure is 100% line-endings, 0% content. Every one of this branch's eight touched paths passes the scoped Prettier check under both `--end-of-line crlf` and `--end-of-line auto`. This branch does not modify `.gitattributes`, Prettier configuration, Git configuration, or the line endings of any file.

  **8. F7 — Resend caret range (pre-existing; outside this branch's authorized scope).** `apps/web/package.json` declares `"resend": "^6.22.1"` (a caret range), introduced with the dependency in PR #61 (D-035), whereas D-035 §4 states the package should be pinned to an exact version with no caret range. This predates all Stage 5 and D-038 work. `apps/web/package.json` is not within this branch's authorized file scope (§13); the caret is recorded here as a known, separately-tracked issue and is not changed by this branch. Runtime risk is negligible while `pnpm-lock.yaml` and CI's `--frozen-lockfile` hold the resolved version.

  **9. Deployment gates remain open.** Three gates stay open and are not closed by this entry: (a) **D-037 §12 Hostinger reverse-proxy trusted-source verification**, narrowed by D-038 §6 to apply exclusively to SOURCE-dimension rate limiting on the two activation POST routes — until a verified trusted-header/hop configuration exists, all activation POST traffic shares the single `"unknown-source"` SOURCE bucket, an acknowledged application-wide throttling exposure; (b) **request-body-logging confirmation** — verification that Hostinger/proxy/origin request-body logging is disabled, or does not capture, `/api/activation/continue` and `/api/activation/activate` specifically, since both requests carry the raw token in their JSON body at redemption time; (c) **Resend fragment-preservation** — verification, against Resend's own current official documentation or dashboard configuration, that automated-delivery link handling (click-tracking, link-rewriting, redirects) preserves a URL fragment intact, or else disabling that behavior for activation emails, or else keeping automated delivery unavailable. Gate (c) is presently moot: automated delivery is disabled in every environment (`EMAIL_DELIVERY_ENABLED` defaults to `"false"`, and `isAutomatedDeliveryEnabled()` additionally requires `RESEND_API_KEY` and `EMAIL_FROM`), so it is a must-verify-before-enabling condition, not a live exposure. **Activation must not be exposed in staging or production until gates (a) and (b) are verified and documented, and gate (c) additionally before automated email delivery is enabled.**

  **11. Stage 5f disposition and supersession of D-038's status.** The D-034 Stage 5f validation contract requires each of the following §10 validations to pass: `pnpm test`, `pnpm --filter web run test:integration`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm --filter web run test:e2e`, `pnpm --filter web run test:e2e:verify-artifact-safety`, the scoped Prettier check over this branch's eight changed files (`--end-of-line auto`), and `git diff --check`. The canonical `pnpm format:check` is **not** part of this pass condition — its F6 failure is a repository-wide Windows CRLF working-tree line-ending condition (§7), reviewed and accepted for D-039 on the grounds recorded in the disposition paragraph below.

  **Disposition (September 2, 2026).** D-039 is **Accepted**. All functional, security, database-backed, build, unit, integration, browser-E2E, artifact-safety, lint, type-check, scoped-formatting, and diff validations passed (§10): `pnpm test` (2,913 passed / 110 skipped / 0 failed), `pnpm --filter web run test:integration` (9 files / 110 tests passed against the real local `heritage_v3_test`), `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm --filter web run test:e2e` (3 tests passed), `pnpm --filter web run test:e2e:verify-artifact-safety` (PASS — probe genuinely failed at its own assertion, canary absent everywhere scanned), the scoped Prettier check on all eight changed files with `--end-of-line auto`, and `git diff --check` — each exit 0 — with clean database and artifact behaviour confirmed. **The canonical local `pnpm format:check` command did not pass** (exit 1): the Windows checkout contains repository-wide CRLF working-tree line endings, and Prettier's default `endOfLine: "lf"` flags them. This environment-specific failure was explicitly reviewed and accepted for D-039 because: it affects all 302 tracked formatted files rather than only this branch; it was not introduced by Stage 5; `prettier --check --end-of-line auto .` passed repo-wide; the scoped Prettier checks passed on all eight changed files; and `git diff --check` passed. This entry does **not** claim the canonical `pnpm format:check` command passed, and does **not** describe `prettier --check --end-of-line auto .` as the canonical command.

  **On this accepted entry being committed and merged into `main`:**
  - **D-034 Stage 5 is accepted as technically complete and locally validated** — comprising D-037's Stage 5a–5f and the D-038 fragment-transport remediation (D-038a–d), as implemented on this reconciliation branch's tip.
  - **D-038's temporary "D-034 Stage 5 remains FAIL" status is superseded by accepted D-039** — the single status line in D-038 §10/§11/§Effect.
  - **D-034 Stage 6 development becomes procedurally unblocked** — the D-038 §10 ordering gate (D-038d → Stage 5f → Stage 6) is cleared. Stage 6 implementation still requires its own separately reviewed Decisions-Log contract entry under D-034 §15's guarded-checkpoint discipline; **D-039 does not provide that entry and does not authorize Stage 6 implementation.**
  - **Activation remains blocked from staging and production exposure** by the three open §9 deployment gates (Hostinger reverse-proxy trusted-source verification; request-body-logging confirmation for `/api/activation/continue` and `/api/activation/activate`; Resend fragment-preservation before automated email is enabled). **D-039 does not close any of those deployment gates.**

  **12. Stage 6 gating.** D-034 Stage 6 (CLIENT-role post-login routing and the production-quality `/client` Client Home / Overview) becomes **development-unblocked once this accepted entry is committed, merged, and pulled into `main`**. This entry does not itself unblock Stage 6, does not begin Stage 6 work, and does not constitute the Stage 6 implementation contract — that remains a separate future Decisions-Log entry under D-034 §15's own guarded-checkpoint discipline. D-038 §10's ordering (D-038d → Stage 5f → Stage 6) is honored, not shortened or reordered.

  **13. Authorized file scope of this branch.** Exactly eight paths: `docs/HERITAGE_V3_DECISIONS_LOG.md` (this entry); `apps/web/src/features/activation/source.ts` (F2, comment-only); and the six F3 test files named in §5. `docs/HERITAGE_V3_TASK_BOARD.md` is not modified. No production runtime code path is altered: F2 is comment-only, F3 is test-only, F4 is contract-text-only with the implementation already conformant.

- **Rationale:** Recording this as a new entry rather than editing D-037 or D-038 in place preserves both as accurate historical records while giving the D-038b/c/d completion reconciliation and the final Stage 5f pass their own durable, evidence-backed home — the identical pattern D-035/D-036/D-038 each used. Making the F2 fix comment-only, the F3 fix test-only, and the F4 fix a documentation supersession of a contract literalism (rather than changing already-correct, already-tested field-level error behavior) keeps this branch's blast radius to governance text and test realism, with zero production runtime change — appropriate for a correction-and-reconciliation pass that must not itself introduce risk. Superseding D-038's "Stage 5 remains FAIL" status only conditionally on a fresh, independently-executed validation record answers the one thing D-038d's history cannot prove from the repository alone (§3), without taking prior claims on trust. Keeping the three deployment gates explicitly open, and stating plainly that activation must not reach staging or production until they are verified, keeps this entry honest about what a code-and-governance pass can and cannot certify.
- **Constraint:** No `schema.prisma` change, no migration, no dependency installation or version change, no environment-file change, and no Git/Prettier/`.gitattributes` configuration change is authorized or performed by this entry. `docs/HERITAGE_V3_TASK_BOARD.md` is not modified. D-034, D-035, D-036, D-037, and D-038 are not edited except for D-038's single "Stage 5 remains FAIL" status line, superseded per §11 once this accepted entry is merged into `main`. The implementation diff for this branch is exactly the eight paths in §13. No existing action name, error code, entity-type constant, route, exported type, or function signature is renamed or removed.
- **Deferred:** F7 (the Resend caret range in `apps/web/package.json`, outside this branch's scope) to a separate change; the three §9 deployment gates to their own pre-staging verification; the D-034 Stage 6 implementation contract to its own future entry; D-034 Stage 7 and Stage 8 to their own later work. `docs/HERITAGE_V3_TASK_BOARD.md` closure of any Phase 3 item remains Stage 8's responsibility.
- **Effect:** As an accepted entry, on being committed and merged into `main`: records D-038b/c/d as complete and merged at `06f04df`; records the F2 and F3 corrections on this branch and the accepted F4 contract correction; supersedes D-038's "D-034 Stage 5 remains **FAIL**" status line; accepts D-034 Stage 5 as technically complete and locally validated (§11); and procedurally unblocks D-034 Stage 6 development while neither providing the Stage 6 implementation contract nor closing the three §9 deployment gates. Does not itself stage, commit, push, open a pull request, modify the Task Board, enable automated email delivery, or authorize any staging or production exposure of activation.
